# PartyTrackerReplayTest: every Hypixel session with party activity in the owner's client logs (2026-02
# to 2026-11, not the legacy-mod sessions whose /party list answers were hidden), reduced to party system
# lines, the mod API party packets and connection events. Names and uuids anonymized (the player is Alice;
# #N = a uuid the logs never named); party, guild and private chat omitted; dividers shortened to -----.
# Times are seconds from the session start.
# <t> chat <text, \n = newline> | <t> info out | <t> info in <Name:ROLE>... | <t> connect | <t> world
